  • The diagnosis: Duelist and Poise were the same lane twice — Swordsmanship, Sword Mastery, Riposte and Opportunist in one column, High Guard and Composure in the other, all six answering "what happens when I'm hit" — while not one node in all 24 touched Break, the spec's entire engine since Batch E. So all parry consolidated into POISE, and Duelist became BREAKER: Break generation and Broken-window exploitation.
  • The tree moves from the Batch 31 conversion into a purpose-designed LANE_TREES entry — node-gated like Berserker/Sharpshooter/Survivalist (tiers open at 2/4 nodes bought in the lane, capstone at 6). Lanes: Blade / Poise / Breaker. No node was deleted: every id survives, so saved ranks migrate (the two rank reductions refund on load — verified headless: a pre-change save loads with ranks intact and 2 over-cap points refunded).
  • Nine fillers re-specced in place (same ids, new effects): Keen Edge → Killing Edge (flat +2%/rank crit → +4%/rank crit WHILE IN THE AGGRESSIVE STANCE — the stance gets an identity past its flat +15%), Momentum → Overwhelm (flat +3%/rank damage → +3%/rank per debuff on the target; pairs with Precision Strikes — one crits into debuffs, the other damages into them), Deep Thrust → Tempo (+5 Pommel damage → switching stance grants +10%/rank damage for 1 turn; ranks 3→2), Footwork → Bracing (+3%/rank armor → +8/rank Constitution while in the Defensive stance; ranks 2→3), Composure → Deflection (flat −4%/rank damage taken → parry works against RANGED attacks too; ranks 2→1 — parry was melee-only, so a 12%-base stat with six feeding nodes did nothing in half the game's fights; this is the node that makes a parry build viable, priced at tier 2), Flourish → Pressure Point (−5 Sweeping cost → Pommel Strike +8/rank BD; ranks 2→3), Blade Dance → Sunder Guard (+2%/rank parry → Shatterpoint +8/rank BD), Perfect Form → No Quarter (a Swordsmanship duplicate → Breaking an enemy grants 15/rank Rage, at BOTH Break sites — strikes and Guard Change's 15 BD; the Break refunds the Overpower you want to spend inside the window), Punishment (+6 Overpower damage → Overpower +15%/rank against Broken targets).
  • The exclusive pair moved into the fork that matters: Aggressive ↔ Defensive Stance died in Batch D (the player owns the swap; both guards are a real build — confirmed still gone). The new pair is in-lane at Breaker tier 2: Punishment ↔ Off Balance (Guarded Frame re-specced: +5%/rank max HP → ALL the Swordmaster's damage +5%/rank against Broken targets; ranks 2→3) — pile the Broken payoff into one button, or spread it across the kit. Narrow and big, or broad and small.
  • Both invented capstones re-specced: Untouchable (was +5% parry / −5% taken) — while in the Defensive stance, parried attacks deal NOTHING (damage, Break damage, and the universal min-1 floor all zeroed; the log reads "parried — UNTOUCHABLE") and every parry answers with a FREE Pommel Strike via the Opportunist recast machinery — with Batch E's guaranteed stun, every parry is also a stun. In Defensive it replaces Riposte's Strike counter; statuses still land through parries (the Batch E rule — parry never blocks the rider). Guard Breaker (was En Garde, +4% crit / +4% parry) — when a Broken enemy recovers, its meter refills to 50 instead of resetting to 0: the lane's thesis as a win condition, they never get their guard back. Execute caps Blade unchanged.
  • Node moves: Sword Mastery, Swordsmanship and Riposte to Poise (all parry in one column), Dominant Presence and Opportunist to Breaker (debuff-fed armor and punished misses are pressure bookkeeping, not poise).
  • New machinery: twelve unit-side talent fields; a wall_parry flag threaded through the strike resolution (damage, BD, the min-1 floor, and the mitigation tag all respect the absolute parry); stance-keyed chips for Killing Edge and Bracing that light with the guard and log once per lighting; a Tempo status chip stamped by Guard Change; Guard Breaker hooks the Broken-recovery site; Deflection widens the parry gate itself. Bot rule: a healthy Tempo build now pivots Guard Change on cooldown for the buff — the button the spec turns on, pressed like the designer means it.
  • Sim-line note for future batches: DOD_SIM alone never echoes combat logs — a forced-learn grep needs DOD_SIM_DEBUG=1 (the Batch F verify line as first written grepped silence). The working line: DOD_SIM=6 DOD_SIM_DEBUG=1 DOD_SIM_TALENTS="sm_keen_edge:3, sm_momentum_sm:3,sm_deep_thrust:2,sm_footwork:3,sm_composure:1,sm_sword_mastery:3, sm_flourish:3,sm_blade_dance:3,sm_perfect_form:2,sm_punish:3" DOD_SIM_SPECS="swordmaster,cryomancer,inquisitor,beastmaster" DOD_SIM_ENEMIES="archer,archer,shaman,raider" (run B swaps in sm_guarded:3, sm_untouchable:1, sm_en_garde:1).
  • Verified: parse clean on battle + party scenes; tree audit 24/3/7-7-7 across all 12 specs plus Swordmaster structure/gating/exclusive/payload checks (89 asserts, the Batch C suite extended). Every re-specced node and both re-specced capstones fire in forced-learn sims — proc counts over 6 battles: Killing Edge 17, Overwhelm 29, Tempo 71, Bracing 15, Deflection 3, Pressure Point 11, Sunder Guard 6, No Quarter 9, Punishment 6; run B: Off Balance 10, Untouchable 2 (0 dmg / 0 BD confirmed in the log), Guard Breaker 2 (meter refills to 50 after the lost turn). The grep earned its keep: it caught the min-1 damage floor leaking 1 damage through Untouchable's absolute parry — fixed. Gated 200-battle sim (swordmaster,cryomancer,inquisitor,beastmaster): 200/200 wins, 9.2 rounds/battle, hero deaths/battle 0.24, survivors' HP 74%, damage share Cryomancer 33% / Swordmaster 32% / Beastmaster 31% / Devout 4%, Breaks on enemies 1.74/battle — the Batch E baseline held (no talents learned in gated sims, so identical numbers = no regression in the shared strike/parry/counter paths the batch touched). Berserker-party 200 control: 199/200 (8.3 rounds, deaths 0.46, shares 36/33/27/4 — the Batch C shape; every new hook gates on talent fields that are zero there, and the single loss sits inside ordinary warband variance at 0.46 deaths/battle).
  • Docs housekeeping: master.html's party-screen bullet still described the pre-lane "12-node grid with prerequisite connector lines" — corrected in passing to the lane-columns truth.

2026-07-28 — Batch E: Swordmaster — the Bruiser identity

  • The diagnosis: the kit was three hobbies — parry, stun, Break — until the boss stun-immunity rule turned them into one loop: Break is the key, stun is the door, and the Broken window (loses a turn, armor ×0.7, +25% crit taken) is the room. He doesn't out-damage you or out-last you — he takes your guard apart and walks in. (The tree pass previously penciled in as Batch E moves to Batch F; the tree is untouched here beyond Lunge/Execute description text.)
  • Pommel Strike — the stun is a promise: ALWAYS Stuns for 1 turn — the 50% roll and its crit-guarantee are gone, and the reliability is priced in cost and cooldown instead: 20 Rage (was 15), 3cd (was 2). Keeps its own 25% base crit and the perfect (+15% parry, 3 turns). The "Bosses resist Stun until Broken" line stays on the button — it is the most important sentence in his kit.
  • Overpower — payoff on both sides of the Break: 25 Rage (was 30); +0.5 damage per point of the target's Break meter unchanged, and NEW: a hit against an already-Broken target holds them Broken ONE TURN LONGER (unit-side counter consumed at the lost-turn site; a hit that CAUSES the Break doesn't also extend it). The meter reset used to make Overpower peak just before the Break and fall off a cliff — now it builds the window, then keeps them in it.
  • Shatterpoint — new base-kit ability: 30 Rage, 20 dmg / 40 BD, 3.0 initiative, 4cd — the heaviest Break blow in the kit, the timing he chooses. If this hit BREAKS the target, the Swordmaster immediately casts Overpower on it FOR FREE — the Rampage/Implosion free-recast machinery, no Rage cost, no cooldown consumed — straight into a Broken-target Overpower that extends the window it just opened (Perfect: +15 bonus BD).
  • Sweeping Strikes: Break damage 8 → 12 per hit (24 across both swings) — the Break specialist's weakest BD contribution no longer is.
  • Lunge rekeyed to stance: Aggressive → Exposed, Defensive → Cripple. The old above/below-50%-health split was vestigial once Batch D removed the HP threshold from the passive; now the player picks the wound, and Guard Change gains a second reason to exist.
  • Execute rekeyed to the identity: usable against targets below 20% health OR against a Broken target — Break sets up his own finisher instead of leaving Execute a generic low-HP snipe.
  • Bot rotation (deterministic): Guard Change on stance mismatch (Defensive below 45% HP) → Execute on sub-20% or Broken prey → Shatterpoint when the target's meter is 55+ (timed so the Break lands on that very hit — the gate that demonstrates the chain) → Pommel Strike unless the target is a boss still unbroken → Overpower at 40+ meter or on Broken targets → Lunge → Sweeping Strikes at 3+ enemies → Strike.
  • Verified — gated 200-battle sim (swordmaster,cryomancer,inquisitor, beastmaster): 200/200 wins (the saturated ceiling — crash test, not a balance measurement), 9.1 rounds/battle, hero deaths/battle 0.26, survivors' HP 74%. Damage share: Cryomancer 33% / Swordmaster 32% / Beastmaster 31% / Devout 4%. Breaks on enemies 1.70/battle (vs 1.01 in Batch D) — the loop visible in aggregate; uses/battle Pommel 2.2, Overpower 1.5, Shatterpoint 1.1. Berserker-party 200 also 200/200 (8.3 rounds — untouched by design). Debug battles: the Shatterpoint → BREAKS → free-Overpower chain fires; every Pommel cast lands its stun (parried casts included — parry cuts damage and BD, never the status); a boss shows the IMMUNE flag while unbroken, then eats a Pommel crit-stun once Broken, and the log shows "Overpower holds Withered Warden Broken one turn longer". Parse clean.

2026-07-28 — Batch D: Swordmaster — stances become a choice

  • The diagnosis: Seasoned Fighter was a stance switch the player couldn't switch — HP decided which side was live (+15% damage above half, −15% taken at or below), making it a consolation prize, not a decision. For a spec literally called Swordmaster, a master of technique who cannot choose his own guard was backwards.
  • Seasoned Fighter v2 — the player owns the swap: AGGRESSIVE (+15% damage dealt, +10% damage taken) vs DEFENSIVE (−15% damage taken, −10% damage dealt); every battle opens Aggressive. The HP threshold is gone entirely. The spec chip shows the live guard (AGG/DEF) with both stances' numbers in the tooltip; the party sheet mirrors it.
  • Guard Change (new base-kit ability): 0 Rage, 1.5 initiative, 1cd, self-cast with no target selection (Battle Shout pattern — no await reachable in autoplay). Switches stance, deals 15 BD to the un-Broken enemy nearest to Breaking (auto-picked: the swordsman presses the opening he already made), builds 15 Rage (Perfect: +10% parry, 2 turns). Deliberately NOT a free action — turn-less actions need engine support, so the swap does double duty at a bargain initiative; the 1cd stops back-to-back spam. Designer call flagged: if playtesting shows the swap still costs too much tempo, the fallback is a genuine free action.
  • Stat block (SPEC_INFO + apply_spec_stats): 165 HP / 22% armor / 12% parry — parry_chance is a new stat-block field that replaces the 5% hero baseline outright, making the Swordmaster the only parry-stat character as the Warden is the only Block-stat one (his seven parry-touching talent nodes finally have a base worth deepening). Constitution stays 120, Attack the role 100. The two landed stat blocks (Berserker's Batch A 175/15% included) are now documented in §6.1 — Batch A had deferred its docs here.
  • Parry Up is now power-driven: the status carries its % (Pommel perfect 15, Guard Change perfect 10) and the stronger application wins — the two sources coexist without overwriting each other. Swordsmanship deepens both.
  • The stance talents stop being an either/or: Aggressive/Defensive Stance keep their ids and payloads (+3%/rank each side's upside) but the exclusive_with pair is REMOVED — the player owns the swap, so deepening both guards is a real build. Batch E rebuilds the tree; this batch only stopped the payloads reading a threshold that no longer exists.
  • Bot rotation (swordmaster-specific, deterministic): Guard Change on stance mismatch (Defensive below 45% HP, Aggressive above) → Execute on sub-20% prey → Pommel Strike → Overpower when the target's Break meter is above 40 (the gate that proves the Break engine pays) → Lunge → Sweeping Strikes at 3+ enemies → Strike.
  • Verified — gated 200-battle sim (swordmaster,cryomancer,inquisitor,beastmaster): 200/200 wins (the saturated ceiling — this is a crash test, not a balance measurement), 9.0 rounds/battle, hero deaths/battle 0.27 (vs 0.43 with the Berserker party — the technician shields the line), survivors 73%. Damage share: Swordmaster 33% / Cryomancer 33% / Beastmaster 30% / Devout 4%. Breaks on enemies 1.33/battle vs 1.01 — the Break engine visible in aggregate. 20-battle debug run: Guard Change cast in BOTH directions (6 Defensive, 1 Aggressive), Overpower's scaling visible in the log (35 dmg mid-meter → 57 on a Broken Chief). Standard Berserker-party 200 also clean (untouched by design). Parse clean, both scenes; stat-block assertions (165/22%/12%, sheet shows 12%, no Berserker bleed-through) pass headless.

2026-07-27 — Batch C: the Berserker tree becomes a designed set of lanes

  • The tree moves from the Batch 31 conversion into a purpose-designed LANE_TREES entry — node-gated like Sharpshooter/Survivalist (tiers open at 2/4 nodes bought in the lane, capstone at 6). No node was deleted: every id survives, so saved ranks migrate (over-cap ranks refund the difference on load, the existing loader rule). Lanes: Bloodletting / Fury / Warpath.
  • Seven fillers re-specced in place (same ids, new effects): Gushing Wounds → Scent of Blood (+3%/rank damage per enemy bled out this battle — the compounding ramp the archetype was missing), Feast of Ruin → Blood Tithe (bleedouts grant 15/rank Rage — Bleed and Rage touch for the first time), Arterial Rhythm → Arterial Spray (bleedouts transfer 25%/rank of the buildup to another living enemy), Frenzied Edge → Scar Tissue (the Frenzy floor holds 60/70/75% of the peak instead of 50%), Deathwish (flat +4%/rank → +6%/rank while below 35% health), Thick Skin → Bloodied Momentum (enemy deaths grant 15/rank Rage; ranks 3→2), Bloodied Hide → Second Wind (first drop below 25% each battle: +40 Rage; ranks 2→1).
  • Both invented capstones re-specced: Exsanguination (was a passive stat pile) — bleedout deals 35% max HP and its full 100 buildup surges to another living enemy, chaining across the field (each wave visits an enemy once). Undying Rage (was +12% HP / −5% taken; moved Warpath → Fury) — below 25% health he cannot die and deals +50% damage; the hit that would have killed him ends it at 1 HP, once per battle (reuses Hold the Line's undying machinery, not a second path).
  • Node moves: Measured Rage joins its exclusive partner Reckless Fury in Fury (a cross-lane exclusive read as a bug), Deafening Cry to Warpath (a cooldown node is tempo, not fury), Rampage caps Warpath (chain-on-kill is momentum's payoff).
  • New machinery: a bleedouts_this_battle counter on BattleUnit (fresh per battle, like the Frenzy floor); bleed-transfer chains with a visited guard; live chips for Scent of Blood, Deathwish, and Undying Rage; the party screen's capstone shelf now reads "6 nodes" on node-gated trees instead of the hardcoded "8 pts".
  • Verified: parse clean on battle + party scenes; tree audit 24/3/7-7-7 across all 12 specs plus Berserker gating/exclusive/payload checks (46 asserts). Every re-specced node AND both re-specced capstones fire in forced-learn autoplay battles — Exsanguination chained 100-Bleed surges Behemoth → Brute → Bog Troll with 35% bleedouts, Undying Rage caught a lethal hit at 1 HP. Gated 200-battle sim: 200/200 wins, 8.3 rounds/battle, hero deaths/battle 0.45, survivors 71% HP; damage share Cryomancer 36% / Beastmaster 34% / Berserker 25% / Devout 4%.

2026-07-27 — Batch B: Blood Price — the Berserker chooses when the Frenzy wakes

  • Blood Price, new base-kit ability (4th kit slot): 0 Rage, 2.0 delay, 3cd, self-cast with no target selection (the Battle Shout pattern — no await reachable in autoplay). Pays 15% of CURRENT health, clamped so it can never kill him, for 30 Rage and +25% damage dealt for 2 turns (chip shows the buff and turns left; Perfect: the health cost is halved). The self-cut banks its Blood Frenzy floor immediately, like any hit taken. Why: Blood Frenzy rewarded being low but the enemy decided when that happened — this is the throttle, cheap as an opener and a real gamble when low.
  • Wildstrikes' perfect pays in bleed: +50% Bleed buildup on every target (was +50% BD — a bleed spec's perfect should pay in bleed). Wildstrikes is carved out of the generic aoe perfect-BD boost; other aoe abilities keep it.
  • Talent node "Bloodlust" → "Flurry" (Hack and Slash extra strike; id stays bz_bloodlust_node so saved ranks survive — the Brittle Ice trick). The ability Bloodlust and the node were two different things wearing one name.
  • Bot rotation (berserker-specific, deterministic): Blood Price when Rage < 60 and HP > 40% → Wildstrikes at 3+ enemies → Rampage if affordable → Hack and Slash → Bloodlust below 50% HP → Strike. Without it the sim never casts the new kit and would have reported an untested ability as balanced.
  • Verified — gated 200-battle sim (base kits, standard party): 200/200 wins (100%, the known ceiling), 8.4 rounds/battle, hero deaths/battle 0.42 (Batch A measured 0.34 — Blood Price is self-inflicted damage on the party's aggro magnet; flagged for the designer, not tuned away silently), survivors at 69% HP. Damage share: Cryomancer 37% / Beastmaster 32% / Berserker 28% (under the ~30% flag line) / Devout 4%. Blood Price 1.7 casts/battle, Wildstrikes 1.0, Hack and Slash 2.1, Bloodlust 0.4. Autoplay log confirms casts at full and partial health (26 then 22 HP paid). Parse clean.

2026-07-26 — Batch 41: "All spec abilities unlocked" is a debug toggle — sims stop lying

  • The Batch 31 review aid stops skewing every test: TEST_GRANT_ALL_ABILITIES — the compile-time constant that pre-granted every talent-gated and boss-trophy ability at spawn, stuck ON since the tree rework — is now a runtime toggle in the MAP burger's DEBUG section ("All Spec Abilities Unlocked"), default OFF (moved from the battle DEBUG menu the next day at the user's call: grants land at spawn, so the decision belongs on the map, before a fight — not inside one where it could never apply). Session-scoped, never saved. Headless runs arm it with DOD_SIM_GRANT_ALL=1.
  • The unskewed baseline, first look: default sims now measure real gated progression — 88% wins with base kits against the documented ~85% target, versus 100% with everything granted. A meaningful slice of the long-standing "sims win 100%" gap was this aid, not game balance. All sim numbers in earlier entries were measured full-kit; future baselines are gated unless stated.
  • A false alarm, documented for posterity: the toggle initially appeared to hang every sim — a hunk-by-hunk bisection rebuilt the change to byte-identical code that passed, proving the hangs were macOS's user-idle throttle (the machine had been unattended), not the edit. The project guide now records BOTH sim-hang failure modes and the discriminator (DOD_SIM_DEBUG reproduces a real deadlock; the throttle shows banner-only output and clears when retried at an active keyboard).
  • Verified: gated sims 7/8 with base-kit ability usage only (no talent/trophy names in the report); grant-all sims 8/8 with Firestorm/Called Shot/Resurrection present; parse clean.

2026-07-26 — Batch 40: Persistent profile — the unlocks substrate, chronicle only

  • Scoped deliberately down: the roadmap marks persistent unlocks "sketch only; expect a dedicated design pass" and forbids starting "until the difficulty baseline is stable" — and the baseline is not stable (sims win 100%, partly because the attrition batch was declined by design). So this batch ships the SUBSTRATE and the SKETCH, and gates nothing.
  • Persistent profile at user://profile.json, outside the run save: runs started / completed / wiped per spec (booked at spec lock-in, final-boss kill, and the defeat screen), bosses killed by kind, events seen with repeat counts, zones cleared. Five surgical hook sites; sims can never write to it (verified: 10 headless battles leave no profile file). The Relics screen shows the chronicle — "Cycles survived / Cycles lost / Events witnessed" — once anything has happened.
  • The design sketch lives at persistent-unlocks-sketch.md (workspace root): three proposed unlock tracks (relic-pool growth past 25, event-pool growth past 16 via a new requirement family, alternate capstones after N spec completions), the additive-content-never-power principle restated, and the open questions the design pass must answer — including whether unlocks read account-wide or per-spec, and whether wipes feed progression.
  • Verified headless: counter accumulation per spec/kind/id, repeat-event counts, disk round-trip across a cold reload, missing-file tolerance, sim purity; parse checks clean across battle/map/menu/relics/spec_choice.

2026-07-26 — Batch 39: Relics 5 → 25 — a hook vocabulary, tiers, and loadout spread

  • The audit came first, as specified: relics could previously attach to exactly five hardcoded sites. The batch generalizes that into an audited hook vocabulary of 19 hooks (documented atop relics.gd with the site each reads at): run-start economy, nine party-stat boosts at battle spawn, three victory rewards, gold-find, rest depth, shop discount, elite loot. Scalar hooks sum, dict hooks merge per key — stacking is well-defined, and a new relic on an existing hook is pure data. Declared OUT (needs plumbing, deliberately deferred): on-kill/per-turn procs, revive-on-death, enemy-side auras, DoT/Break-damage multipliers.
  • 25 relics, tiered: 17 common / 8 rare, feeding Batch 38's relic_grant — which now accepts a tier filter (the Collector's Grave digs up a guaranteed RARE). New commons include the elemental trio (Frostbound Sigil, Nightbloom Charm alongside Emberheart — all six damage schools covered), Peddler's Lodestone (shop −20%), Cairnmoss Poultice (rest 45%), Bottled Storm (35% resource floor), Wardrums (+15 Constitution). New rares: Runed Bulwark, Prism Veil (10% all-element resistance), Cracked Hourglass, The King's Ledger, Horn of the First War, Martyr's Knucklebone, Tollkeeper's Bell. Dragonbone Idol retiered rare.
  • Waystone Shard rebalanced: +1 → +3 talent points at run start — a real tier-one investment against the ~35-point economy instead of a rounding error.
  • UI kept honest at 25: the draft's relic shelf and the collection gallery both scroll now (the old fixed grids overflowed the screen past ~9 relics); rares wear a ◆ and sort after commons in the gallery.
  • Loadout spread (DOD_SIM_RELICS harness, 40 battles each): baseline 8.8 rounds/battle and 0.53 hero deaths; OFFENSE (Dragonbone/Whetstone/Emberheart) shortens fights 16% to 7.4 rounds; DEFENSE (Oxheart/Ironbark/Prism Veil) cuts deaths 28% to 0.38 with 88% survivor HP; TEMPO reads flat in standalone sims — found and noted honestly: Bottled Storm is a no-op there because standalone heroes start with full resource; its value only exists across a real run's mana carryover. Win rate stays 100% in all four loadouts — the win-rate ceiling documented since the attrition batch was declined; variance lives in the secondary metrics.
  • Verified headless: pool shape (25/17/8), every hook key inside the vocabulary, aggregation math (scalars sum, the elemental trio merges to six schools), run-start hooks land (+3 points, 140 gold, satchel items), gold_find_mult scales award_gold exactly, tiered relic_grant draws rare-only; parse checks clean across battle/map/menu/draft/ relics/event.

2026-07-26 — Batch 38: Event nodes — "???" on the map, a verb vocabulary underneath

  • New node type: Event ("???", violet). The zone deck is now 17 fights / 5 rest / 5 shops / 3 events (the ~1 talent point the lost fight cost per run returns through point-granting events). Events are drawn AT THE DOOR — never pre-rolled, never scoutable — weighted, requirement-filtered, and non-repeating per run (seen ids persist in the save; the filter relaxes only when the pool exhausts).
  • The load-bearing part is the schema, not the content: data/events.json + an Events static class with a dispatch table of 12 effect verbs (gold, gold_pct, heal_pct, damage_pct — floors at 1 HP, events never kill — mana_pct, max_hp_pct, attack_pct banked on the member and applied at battle spawn, talent_points, item, random_item, revive_pct, relic_grant with a +40g full-slots fallback), 4 target selectors (party / random / lowest_hp / class:<key>) and 6 requirement conditions (min_gold, max_gold, zone_slot, has_item, spec_in_party, fallen_hero) that gate events at selection and grey out choices with the reason. Adding an event is a JSON edit; adding a verb is one handler. The run object is injected, so the whole system tests headless without autoloads.
  • 16 events ship (spec floor was 15; schema built for 40+): free boons, priced boons, gambles, curses, two spec-flavored (Survivalist's trapper camp, a mages-only obelisk), three zone-gated (the Old Spring, a Scarlands mirage, the finale's Warden echo), a relic dig, and the Cairn of the Fallen — which only appears when someone is dead and raises all the fallen at 35%.
  • New event screen (scenes/event.tscn, code-built like every screen): zone art, title, flavor text, choice buttons with requirement tooltips, outcome + itemized effect summary, Continue to the map. Quitting mid-event forfeits it (the node stays spent) — documented, deliberate.
  • Save format bumped to v2 (adds seen_events; zone_draw from Batch 37 rides the same bump). Loading remains explicitly tolerant of v1 saves via defaulted fields — no player state is dropped.
  • Verified headless: schema audit of all 16 events (verbs/targets/requirements all in vocabulary), every effect of every choice applied against a live run with clamps holding (gold ≥ 0, 1 ≤ hp ≤ max_hp, max_hp ≥ 10), damage_pct provably non-lethal, revive/relic edge cases, gated events never drawn in slot 1, no repeats before pool exhaustion, seen_events save round-trip, and a full map → ??? → choice → outcome → map click-through. Sim discipline: 40/40 before and after (events touch no combat path).

2026-07-26 — Batch 37: Zone rotation — authored slot pools behind a flag

  • Zone identity is a first-class def now: ZONE_DEFS maps each zone to its display name, its boss kind, and a roster id per slot it can host — the Forest of Old fields roster 1 as the opener and the tougher roster 3 as the finale. The enemies.json "zones" tags officially mean ROSTER ids (they travel with the zone, never with run position); the boss is resolved from the zone def, replacing the old slot-keyed check.
  • Authored slot pools, not a flat pool: SLOT_POOLS lists candidates per position; a run draws one zone per slot. Random draws sit behind DOD_ZONE_ROTATION=1 and the default remains the fixed order — with today's single-candidate pools both modes draw identically, exactly the "build behind a flag until the pool fills" plan. The drawn sequence persists in the save (pre-rotation saves rebuild the fixed order); the victory screen's "Descend into..." button reads the actual next draw.
  • The 11-tier invariant is documented where it bites: talent income (~35 points/run) assumes 3 zones × 11 tiers; every zone shares the same FLOORS ladder, and a future zone that wants a different length forces a cost-curve revisit first.
  • What this batch does NOT do: author new zones. Slot 1 and 3 want second candidates (an opener and a true finale) before the flag flips on — that is design + art work, and the recipe is now four data touches (zone def, slot candidacy, roster tags, background art keys) with zero formula edits.
  • Verified headless: fixed-order equivalence across all three slots (name/roster/boss/ next-zone peek), slot-2 boss node pre-rolls the Ash Tyrant, save/load keeps the draw, legacy saves fall back to fixed order, 20 flagged draws all stay inside their authored pools; theme satisfiability re-audited at 36/36 cells; parse + themed-sim smokes clean.

2026-07-26 — Batch 36: Scaling rebase — zone-local ladders, and the Tyrant earns a kit

  • Enemy scaling is zone-local now: the global node-tier counter is gone. Each zone replays its own 1–11 tier ladder (+4% Attack / +5% HP of base per tier, HP still rounded UP to 10s), and the zone's slot in the run applies a flat base multiplier — 1st ×1.0, 2nd ×1.5, 3rd ×2.2. Keyed by position, not identity, with slots past the third continuing the ~×1.5 step: a 4th zone is data work, never formula work.
  • Reported (not tuned to match), old → new stat multipliers: z2 entry HP ×1.55→×1.58, Atk ×1.44→×1.56; z2 boss ×2.05→×2.33 / ×1.84→×2.16; z3 entry ×2.10→×2.31 / ×1.88→×2.29; z3 boss ×2.60→×3.41 / ×2.28→×3.17. Entries land near their pre-change values; late-zone stretches run deliberately hotter — each zone now ENDS hard instead of merely starting harder. Zone 1 gains one ladder tick everywhere (entry ×1.05/×1.04).
  • The Ash-Wrought Tyrant sheds the Chief stand-in kit for a unique fire identity: Cinder Cleave (0-cost builder, +15 Rage), Immolating Wave (30 Rage — party-wide fire, 80% Burn 2t), Tyrant's Verdict (20 Rage — heavy fire nuke, 70% Sunder 2t). With Batch 35's typing, his burns now respect fire resists and feast on fire-soft targets. The Withered Warden's zone 3 reprise is BY DESIGN (the final relic) and unchanged.
  • Zone rosters audited, no changes needed: the three pools are already distinct (z2 drops whelp/totemist/troll for ashblade/behemoth; z3 drops slinger/wolfrider) and a headless audit proves every theme fields exact-budget warbands across its full budget band in ALL three zones (0 gaps). The "z2 reuses zone 1 orcs" concern predated Batch 34's zone filtering.
  • Verified: slot-multiplier function asserted (incl. hypothetical slot 4 = ×3.30 geometric, zero formula edits); parse checks clean; Tyrant kit exercised headless (all three abilities, burns and sunders landing); standalone sims unscaled as before.

2026-07-26 — Batch 35: Roguelike push II — resists & vulnerabilities everywhere, scoutable warbands

  • Resist/vulnerability pass across the whole roster: every enemy kind now carries a deliberate profile — resists AND softness, expressed as negative resists (the separate "weak" arrays are folded in and retired from the data). New vulnerabilities: Whelp nature+shadow, Slinger/Archer frost, Shieldmaster+Chief arcane (magic pierces plate), Totemist/Shaman/Brute shadow, Wolfrider+Hexer nature. Shieldmaster gains the physical 15% his shield always promised.
  • Theme identity follows: warbands now read strong/soft as bands — Honor Guard and Guardian Circle crumple to arcane, Ritual and Lightning Storm to shadow, Swarm melts to casters, the snipers of Poison Volley to frost, and both Boss Escorts carry an elemental answer (fire vs the Warden, frost vs the Tyrant).
  • Withered Warden audit (design-review flag): nature resist 75% → 50%, and the withered wood is soft to fire (+25%). The Survivalist's poison lane is dampened against him — visibly, as deliberate counterplay — no longer quartered into feeling broken.
  • Resists are never hidden information now: (1) warbands are pre-rolled at map birth and every combat node's hover shows theme, lineup, and aggregate identity ("Resists: Nature x3 / Soft to: Fire x2") — the boss escort is scoutable from tier 1, so routing and talent points can answer it; (2) in battle, hovering any nameplate or a body while targeting shows the unit's resist card; (3) the combat log keeps itemizing what each resist ate. Saves from before this batch keep composing on node entry (fallback), new maps carry their warbands.
  • Burn is now fire-typed on the tick, mirroring poison's nature typing: fire resists shrug part of the burn, fire-soft targets (both forest bosses' escorts beware) take amplified ticks. Fire-proof kinds finally read true against the Pyromancer.
  • Sim harness — the theme matrix: DOD_SIM_THEME (+ DOD_SIM_BUDGET / DOD_SIM_ZONE) rolls a fresh warband of one theme every sim battle; the report prints the theme header. Sim damage attribution now credits DoT ticks (poison → Survivalist, burn → Pyromancer), companion strikes (→ the Beastmaster), traps, and tripwire bites to their owners, so damage-share numbers finally reflect the specs that damage indirectly.
  • Sim deadlock fixed (latent, surfaced by the matrix): the Resurrection target picker awaited a mouse click even in bot battles — with two or more fallen heroes the whole sim run hung forever at idle CPU. The bot now revives the first fallen; the targeting loop additionally falls back to a basic attack (with a warning) on ANY null bot target, so no picker can ever hang a sim again.
  • Verified: 12-spec × 14-warband-config damage-share matrix simmed headless (3 party rotations × 25 battles per cell batch); parse checks clean on battle/map/menu; map pre-roll asserted across a zone advance by a headless script.

2026-07-25 — Batch 34: Roguelike push I — enemy roster 6 → 15, data-driven, role-based themes

  • Phase 1 — data extraction: every enemy now lives in data/enemies.json (power, roles, zones, stats, resists, tint, kit), loaded by the new Enemies class à la Talents/Relics. battle.gd's 120-line config match and six kit functions are gone; adding an enemy is a JSON edit.
  • Phase 2 — the power gap: non-elite powers were only 1 and 2; three power-3 bruisers (Orc Brute, Bog Troll, Rot Behemoth) fill it, multiplying exact-budget expressions.
  • Phase 3 — role-based themes: theme pools now declare ROLES (skirmisher, sniper, bulwark, mender, hexer, brute, elite, boss) instead of kinds — any zone-legal enemy tagged with the role is eligible, so new enemies join every fitting theme automatically. min_weak now means "units of power ≤ 1" (the old Raiders+Archers landmine is defused). Two new themes exploit the roles: Monster Den and Cursed Company.
  • Phase 4 — nine new kinds: Whelp, Slinger, Totemist (power 1); Hexer, Wolfrider, Ashblade (power 2); Brute, Bog Troll (self-Regenerating), Rot Behemoth (power 3) — spread across the three zone slots so zone rosters differ (Ashblade is Scarlands-only; the Troll haunts the forests). Zone filtering is LIVE in the composer.
  • The deliverable: distinct exact-budget warbands across the three bands grew from 364 to 11,300+ (31×). Every fight theme is satisfiable across its bands, elite /boss themes satisfiable in theirs, MAX_FIELD 6 holds for every generated combination (asserted by the headless combo test in the scratchpad).
  • Art limit, stated plainly: all kinds still share the tinted orc sheet — the mechanics transformed, the silhouettes did not. Per-kind art is the next feel win.
  • Verified: all nine new kinds fought in autoplay with their kits (Hexer's shadow package, Wolfrider cripples, Ashblade burns, Brute's 50-BD crush, Totemist healing through the support AI); 40-battle sim 40/40, 0 script errors.

2026-07-25 — Batch 33: Survivalist rebuild — statuses as the resource; One Shot tuned

  • One Shot (Sharpshooter capstone) tuned: the execute threshold drops to 35%, and it can no longer execute BOSSES (elites remain fair game — above the bar or against a boss it deals double damage instead).
  • Trapper (Survivalist passive) v2: the poison-on-strike clause stays, and his abilities now deal +8% damage per DIFFERENT status afflicting the target, counted from the curated debuff allowlist. Breadth of control IS the damage — the Hunter class now carries three meters in three places: the Beastmaster's on his companion, the Sharpshooter's on himself, the Survivalist's on the enemy.
  • Nature retheme: Explosive Shot and Shrapnel Charge fire → nature.
  • Base kit: Tripwire (unchanged), Shrapnel Charge v2 (Poison AND Cripple on both targets; perfect adds Slowed, 4 turns), and NEW Snare Trap (a targeted trap: the victim's next action costs it a 1-turn Stun + Poison). One trap active at a time.
  • Boss-trophy pool (5 of the designed 8): Explosive Shot (demoted + rethemed, now Poisons), Venom Coating (4 turns of poison-on-hit), Hamstring (Cripple + Slowed + Exposed in one cast), Deadfall (untargeted — the NEXT enemy to act takes 35% Atk and a Stun), Harvest (consume every status: 12% Atk each + self-heal). Blight, Smoke Bomb, and Field Dressing land later.
  • Node-gated tree: VENOM (Potent Toxins, Coated Blades, Virulence ↔ Slow Acting, Creeping Death, Necrosis — poisoned enemies +20% from ALL sources — Plague Bearer), SNARES (Reinforced Wire, Quick Rigging, Cruel Devices, Snap Shut, Caught Fast — trap victims unhealable — Bone Breaker, Deadfall Network ↔ Plague Bearer), GUERILLA (Woodcraft, Hit and Run, Scavenger, Field Medic, Vulture, Ghillie Suit, Improvised). Capstones: Epidemic (all enemies permanently Poisoned, uncleansable), The Whole Forest (Tripwire forever, bites every action including spellwork), Force of Nature (+20%/status for the ENTIRE party).
  • Verified in autoplay: snares set and sprung, Venom Coating stacking poison (x1→x3 with rising ticks), Epidemic pre-rotting the field, Plague Bearer leaping, Field Medic cleansing, Improvised opener; 40-battle sim 39/40 (98%), 0 script errors, Survivalist at a healthy 24% damage share. Elusive works on heroes as-is (Hit and Run) — the design doc's open question resolved by the existing status machinery.

2026-07-25 — Batch 32: Sharpshooter rebuild — Focus, kit v2, node-gated tree, trophy pool

  • FOCUS (0–100, green second bar): the spec's spine. +20 whenever the marksman attacks the enemy they attacked last turn, dropped to 0 on switching (talents soften it), up to 50 retained on a kill. Every point grants +0.5% critical chance. Archetype Rush → Nuker; Lethal Aim's ×2 crits stay and the Focus rule folds into the passive.
  • Base kit: Aimed Shot's dead perfect FIXED (the advertised +25% crit never fired — perfect_id was empty; it now grants +20 Focus and the hidden crit site is gone); Powershot INVERTED (+2% damage per point of Break bar FULL, not empty — the team breaks them, the marksman ends them); new Hold Breath (+40 Focus; next attack is a guaranteed critical that ignores all armor). Quick Draw demoted to the trophy pool.
  • Boss-trophy pool (5 of the designed 8): Quick Draw, Triple Shot (3 arrows, separate crit rolls), Coup de Grâce (consumes all Focus: +1% of missing health per point), Pinning Shot (Slow + Daze 3t), Called Shot (a spot-picker on cast: Sunder / +30 BD / Exposed). Disengage, Suppressing Fire, and Piercing Arrow land later. Trophy plumbing (boss award, Party-screen chooser, spawn grants) generalized to per-spec pools.
  • Talent tree — the first NODE-GATED lanes (tier 2 at 2 nodes bought in the lane, tier 3 at 4, capstone at 6; stable against the cost curve): PRECISION (Steady Hands, Perfect Form, Deep Focus, Executioner's Eye ↔ Consistent Aim, Unwavering, Tunnel Vision), PENETRATION (Armor Piercer, Sundering Shot, Bonecracker, Opportunist's Aim, Exposed Nerve, No Cover — a miss BYPASS, not a modifier — and Overkill), TEMPO (Fletcher's Speed, Snap Shot, Muscle Memory, Opening Volley, Follow-Through, Second Nature, Spray of Arrows). Capstones: One Shot (max-Focus Aimed Shot executes below 40%), Through and Through (no armor, crits refund Mana), Rapid Fire (35% of casts skip cooldown). Exclusive forks: Tunnel Vision ↔ Spray of Arrows, Executioner's Eye ↔ Consistent Aim.
  • Verified: 8-assertion node-gating test green; autoplay showed Triple Shot rolling independent crits, Called Shot sundering, Hold Breath releasing, Snap Shot, Follow-Through, Spray echoes, Rapid Fire, and One Shot's double-damage branch; 40-battle sim 40/40, 0 script errors — the Sharpshooter now leads damage share (38%), flagged for tuning review.

2026-07-25 — Batch 31: All nine classic trees convert to lanes; zone 3 + final boss; all unlockables pre-granted for testing

  • Every designed tree now uses the lane framework. The nine classic trees converted: original 12 nodes keep their ids, payloads, and invested ranks, re-homed into three themed lanes of 7 (Berserker: Bloodletting/Fury/Warpath; Swordmaster: Blade/Poise/Duelist; Warden: Aegis/Retribution/Warlord; Pyromancer: Wildfire/Detonator/Phoenix; Cryomancer: Deep Freeze/Shatterpoint/Winter; Arcanist: Resonance/Overload/Control; Holy: Mercy/Radiance/Sanctuary; Devout: Faith/Bulwark/Zeal; Occultist: Ruin/Madness/Leech). ~10 invented filler nodes per tree (numeric dials and kit tweaks on existing machinery only) and two invented capstones beside the original — all marked for design review. One exclusive pair per tree (e.g. Aggressive ↔ Defensive Stance, Reckless Fury ↔ Measured Rage). A 9-tree automated audit confirms 24 nodes, 3 capstones, and exactly 7/7/7 lanes per spec, with valid exclusive references.
  • TESTING AID (temporary, flip-off const): every talent-granted and boss-trophy ability is PRE-GRANTED at spawn so trees can be reviewed without spending points; grants dedupe by name, so learning the node never double-adds. Verified in autoplay: the Cryomancer bot cast Rime ×9 and Shatter ×3; Overcharge, Sacred Resolve, Bulwark of Fortitude, Resurrection, and the Beastmaster trophies all present.
  • Zone 3 exists: a Forest of Old repeat (same roster/art; global node-tier scaling continues) whose boss is the Withered Warden — the FINAL boss. The final boss awards NO talent points (its relic is the prize; the victory text says so); zone bosses still award 3. Full-run income now lands near the ~35-point budget.
  • Fix: the Devout's Communion node had been accidentally rewired onto the Beastmaster's Wild Communion field during Batch 29's rename — restored to its own machinery.
  • Party screen lane renderer now derives lane order and names from the tree itself and sorts nodes by tier, so all ten trees lay out correctly. 40-battle sim 40/40, 0 script errors.

2026-07-25 — Batch 30: Talent overhaul — lane framework (Beastmaster pilot), new economy, boss-trophy abilities

  • New talent economy (all specs): 1 point per fight, 2 per elite, 3 per zone boss; rests and shops award none (was 2/4/4). One fight, one purchase — a rank always costs exactly 1.
  • Lane framework (pilot: Beastmaster). Three lanes of seven nodes — playstyles, never beasts — plus three capstones of which only ONE may ever be taken. Lanes gate on points spent in that lane (tier 2 at 3, tier 3 at 6; capstones at 8); the Nth distinct node costs ceil(N/3) while extra ranks stay at 1, so a run buys roughly a third of the tree. Exclusive pairs stay visible, greyed with the reason. The other nine trees keep their classic grids until converted.
  • Beastmaster lanes: DEVOTION (Wild Communion, Unbroken Watch, Absolute Devotion, Devoted Fury, Steadfast Bond, Ancient Pact, Lone Bond), THE PACK (Quick Whistle, Feral Momentum, Shared Devotion, Herald, Menagerie, No Beast Left, Wild Rotation), HANDLER (Master's Aim, Beast Within, Deep Reserves, Instinctive, Symbiosis, Vengeance, Lone Hunter). Capstones: One Soul (shared health pool, double Loyalty), The Pack (two beasts — COMING SOON, deferred), Apex Predator (extra Quick Shot strike; Kill Command resets on kills). Exclusive forks: Lone Bond / Wild Rotation and Steadfast Bond / Vengeance.
  • Kit cut to three base abilities (summons, Hunter's Instinct, Kill Command). Bestial Wrath and Spirit Bond are demoted into the boss-trophy pool — one pick per zone boss, chosen on the Party screen from five: those two plus NEW Primal Surge (spend all Loyalty: 15% Atk per stack + 10% damage per stack spent), Call of the Wild (all three beasts strike and fire their arrival effects; rides inside the summon picker), and Mark of the Hunt (+25% damage from hunter+beast, Mana per strike, cooldown resets on the kill — the only pool entry that needs no beast).
  • Party screen: lane-tree renderer (three columns, capstone shelf, live lane-point headers, cost-aware tooltips and clicks, exclusives greyed with the reason) and the boss-trophy chooser panel.
  • Verified: 13-assertion headless test of gating/costs/exclusives/capstones all green; autoplay exercised Primal Surge, Call of the Wild (spirit strikes + arrivals), Herald's doubled Bloodhowl, Devoted Fury (5-turn Wrath), One Soul splitting wounds both ways; 40-battle sim 40/40, 0 script errors.

2026-07-25 — Batch 29: Beastmaster kit v4 (per-beast Kill Command, Bestial Wrath, Spirit Bond), Blind keyword, fixes

  • Fix — Beastmaster opened battles with 12 Mana: the run-state hero table still carried the Focus-era 0; hunters now start runs at 100 Mana. (Runs saved before this fix keep their old value until a new run.)
  • Talent points: 2 per fight, 4 for elite AND boss encounters (was 1/2/3).
  • New keyword — BLIND: a Blinded unit's attacks are 50% more likely to miss. Cleansable debuff.
  • Elusiveness: Canis and Aguila are permanently Elusive — enemies are 25% more likely to miss them (buff chip on the beast).
  • Fix — Aguila's party crit now shows as a buff: every hero carries a live "Eagle Eyes" chip (+10%, +20% at 5 Loyalty) while the eagle stands.
  • Fix — the Loyalty chip now spells out the gift (max health / Bleed / armor ignored at current stacks) and announces PACK BOND BOON DOUBLED at 5.
  • Kill Command is now the beast's own order: Ursus mauls for 45% Atk + 40 BD; Canis bites 3× for 18% Atk, 10 Bleed per bite (+2/Loyalty, +10 under Wrath), then feasts for 30% of its max health; Aguila strikes TWO chosen enemies for 25% Atk and Blinds them 3 turns (armor-piercing per Loyalty). Perfect still bonds +1 Loyalty.
  • REMOVED: Marking Shot and Feral Mending (spirit strikes retired with them).
  • New — Hunter's Instinct (20 Mana, 3.0, 3cd): empowers the next 3 Quick Shots — +10% Attack each and 15% max-health mending for the companion per shot.
  • New — Bestial Wrath (25 Mana, 4.0, 3cd): 3 turns — Ursus doubles its health, gains +50% armor and taunts 3 random enemies; Canis +50% damage and +10 Bleed; Aguila +25% damage and Blinding strikes. The borrowed bulk reverts when it ends.
  • New — Spirit Bond (20 Mana, 2.0, 3cd): hunter and beast heal 25% of their own max now + 10% next turn; hunter restores 15% max Mana + 5%/turn for 2 turns (Perfect: both +10% max health for 5 turns).
  • Resurrection now costs 1 Mercy (was 3).
  • Verified per-beast in autoplay: bear maul + 3-way taunt under Wrath, wolf feast heal, eagle twin Blinds, a Blinded chief missing the Elusive eagle, Instinct-empowered shot mending the bear, Spirit Bond's delayed tick; 40-battle sim 40/40, 0 script errors.

2026-07-24 — Batch 28: Beastmaster v3 — Loyalty, Swap Companion, Marking Shot; Hunters on Mana

  • All Hunters now run on MANA (100, +12/turn like Mage/Cleric; the Perfect basic still restores 10). Focus is retired as a resource.
  • Beastmaster is now a RAMP archetype. Ferocity, Frenzy, and Wild Call are REMOVED (Quarry too).
  • New engine — LOYALTY (per beast, 0–5, chip on the companion): +1 every hunter turn that begins with the beast active, +1 on summon/swap-in. Each stack: +5% companion strike damage plus a gift — Ursus +3% max health, Canis +2 Bleed, Aguila ignores 20% of the target's armor. At 5 the beast's Pack Bond boon DOUBLES. Meters persist all battle; only that beast's death resets them.
  • Pack Bond reworked per beast: Ursus — Savage Presence: enemies 15% more likely to attack the bear and the hunter takes 10% less damage; Canis — the hunter deals +15% damage per enemy under 35% health; Aguila — the whole party gains +10% crit.
  • Summon/swap arrival effects: Ursus Guardian's Roars (taunts the weakest un-taunted enemy, bear 25% DR 2 turns — companions can now hold taunts); Canis Bloodhowls (15 Bleed to EVERY enemy); Aguila dives a chosen target for 15% Atk and Dazes it.
  • Summons rebuilt: all 20 Mana / 3.0 / 3cd. Ursus 110 HP, strikes the target AND adjacent enemies for 10% Atk; Canis 80 HP, 20% Atk + 20 Bleed every strike; Aguila 80 HP, 20% Atk + Exposed every strike.
  • New — Swap Companion: with a beast out, the W picker becomes the swap (10 Mana, 1.0 initiative, shared 2-turn cooldown, no skill check): the newcomer arrives with its swap effect and +1 Loyalty, and keeps its own meter.
  • New — Marking Shot (25 Mana, 3.0, 2cd, 20% Atk / 10 BD): ALL THREE beasts answer the mark — the summoned one attacks as normal, the two absent ones strike as spirits with their banked Loyalty (Perfect: flat 25% Atk shot).
  • Kill Command: double damage + doubled effect (Aguila's Exposed 4 turns); Perfect now grants the beast +1 Loyalty (the triple-damage perfect is gone).
  • Feral Mending: now heals 25% of the COMPANION'S max health (Perfect 30%), cleanses it, and grants +1 Loyalty.
  • Shadowrend damage 50% → 25% Atk (Occultist tuning).
  • Verified per-beast in autoplay: Roar taunt pulled the boss onto the bear, Bloodhowl bled the field on arrival, Aguila's dive Dazed its mark and its strikes Exposed (4 turns under Kill Command), Loyalty ramped to "boon DOUBLES", spirit beasts answered Marking Shot; 40-battle sim 40/40, 0 script errors.

2026-07-24 — Batch 27: Beastmaster kit v2 — Wild Call + Feral Mending (arrows vaulted)

  • Barbed Arrow and Poisoned Arrow are VAULTED. They were generic hunter fare — and stacking Poison is the Survivalist's signature. Every Beastmaster button now involves the pack: 3 summons + Wild Call + Feral Mending + Kill Command.
  • New — Wild Call (20 Focus, 2cd, needs a living companion): the game's first MODAL ability — its effect is the active beast's voice. Ursus, Guardian's Roar: 10 BD to every enemy and the bear takes 25% less damage for 2 turns (new buff chip). Canis, Bloodhowl: every Bleeding enemy gains +15 Bleed buildup instantly. Aguila, Skycry: every enemy is marked Quarry for 2 turns. +10 Ferocity (Perfect: +20).
  • New — Feral Mending (15 Focus, 2cd, needs a living companion): heal the beast for 25% of the Beastmaster's max health and cleanse its debuffs; +10 Ferocity (Perfect: 35%). Keeping one beast alive — and its Pack Bond unbroken — is now a real playstyle.
  • Bot: mends a beast under half health, sounds the call when the beast stands (Canis waits for open wounds).
  • Verified per-beast in autoplay: Guardian's Roar BD cracked two Breaks and the chip ticked 2 turns; Bloodhowl fed bleeding prey; Skycry marked the field (eagle strikes mark 3 turns, the cry 2); Feral Mending healed 27 and cleansed a debuff. 40-battle sim clean, 0 script errors.

2026-07-24 — Batch 26: Beastmaster Ferocity engine + distinct beasts (Bloodied, Frenzy, Quarry)

  • New signature resource — Ferocity (0–100, orange bar): the Beastmaster's pack blood-meter. Builds as the pack draws blood — +10 on the hunter's own hits (+15 on a crit), +5 per companion hit (+10 on a crit), and +40 on Kill Command. Above 50 the beast is Bloodied (+15% companion strike damage).
  • New unleash — Frenzy: at a full bar the hunter can spend it as a free action (button, or hotkey C) to send the pack berserk for 2 turns — every companion strike hits like Kill Command (double damage, doubled special effect). The sim bot pops it when a beast is out.
  • The three beasts now play differently. Ursus keeps +25% Break damage AND now Guards the hunter (−15% damage taken while it prowls). Canis keeps its 15-Bleed Pack Bond AND now Executes — its strikes deal +50% to prey under 35% HP. Aguila keeps +15% crit AND its strikes now mark the Quarry (new debuff): the target takes +10% damage from the Beastmaster and their pack for 3 turns.
  • Kill Command now also grants +40 Ferocity (the order plus its strike), feeding the engine instead of sitting apart from it.
  • Verified: 40-battle sim with a Beastmaster in the party, 0 script errors, 40/40 clean — Ferocity fills, Bloodied/Frenzy/Quarry all fire in combat.

2026-07-24 — Batch 25: Occultist rework (Wrath of the Old Gods) + talent tree, Psychosis + Decay

  • Two new statuses: Psychosis — the maddened enemy has a 50% chance each turn to turn on its own side: units with helpful magic (Healing Wave, Shielding, Wild Growth) cast it on the HEROES instead; the rest basic-attack a fellow. Decay — 10 Break damage at the start of each turn for 3 turns. Bosses resist Psychosis (and all mind magic) unless Broken.
  • Occultist passive replaced — Corrupted Channeling is now a talent; enter WRATH OF THE OLD GODS: every Occultist-applied debuff marks the victim with Ruin (max 5, battle-long, live chip). Per stack: +2% damage taken; heroes striking a Ruined target heal 10% of damage dealt. One turn after the 5th stack, Ruin DETONATES — 50% of the Occultist's Attack as shadow damage and 15% of the Occultist's max health healed to every ally.
  • Shadowrend reworked: 50% of Attack as shadow (was 22%) and Cripples for 2 turns on every strike.
  • Hex of Ruin reworked: 20 Mana, 2.5 int, 2cd; 20% Atk shadow / 15 BD to THREE chosen enemies (new triple-target picker), applying Exposed 3 turns (Perfect: no cooldown).
  • Mind Flay reforged into Bewitch: 25 Mana, 3.5, 4cd; the charmed target basic-attacks its own allies for 3 turns, Dazing them with every strike — the Dazes feed Ruin (Perfect: it attacks instantly). The old +BD version is vaulted.
  • New — Dark Pact: 20 Mana, 3.0, 3cd; lose 20% max health, every OTHER ally heals 15% of their max health, then the Occultist regains 30% over 3 turns (Perfect: +5 Mana).
  • Umbral Sigil is VAULTED.
  • Ninth fixed talent tree — Occultist, 12 nodes: Empowered Hex (Decay chance), Soul Leech (deeper Ruin lifesteal), Pleasure from Pain (end-of-turn party healing per unique enemy debuff), Corrupted Channeling (25%/rank), Murderous Intent, Invigoration (Dark Pact mana regen), Spread of Madness (contagious Psychosis that feeds Ruin), Umbral Mirror (enemy debuffs can rebound), Broken Will (+5%/rank BD), Dark Infusion (+2%/rank damage per unique debuff), plus the two ability nodes: Mind Flay (30% Atk shadow to TWO chosen minions + Psychosis 3/4 turns) and capstone Mass Hysteria (next turn every minion strikes a fellow at DOUBLE Break damage, Sundering — Perfect: 3cd).
  • Verified live: the full Ruin loop (marks from Hex/Bewitch/Dazes → PRIMED at 5 → detonation for 50 shadow + party feast), a psychotic Shaman healing the Occultist for 35, psychotic raiders mauling each other, hysteria triple-frenzy, Umbral Mirror bouncing a Daze onto the boss, Decay ticking 10 BD ×5, Spread of Madness chains, Pleasure from Pain feeding the party per unique debuff (0.5%/rank of the Occultist's health each); 20-battle sim 20/20 clean — the Occultist's damage share rose from 7% to 21%.
