  • Divine Shield absorbs 30% of the Devout's max health (Perfect: 35%) — was 50/55%.
  • Blessed Barrier converts 4%/rank of absorbed damage into healing (was 3%/rank).
  • Unwavering Faith redesigned (updated tree JSON): now +5%/rank maximum health for the Devout — the per-stack Faith deepening is gone; Faith stays at a flat 3% mitigation / +2% damage per stack.

2026-07-23 — Batch 23: Devout Conviction rework + talent tree, Capacitor trim

  • Holy Capacitor stores 5%/rank of overhealing (was 33%/rank).
  • Devout passive replaced — Devotion Aura is now the "Devoutness" talent (party takes 5%/rank less BD); enter CONVICTION: allies build Faith whenever Divine Shield absorbs damage for them (1 per absorbed hit; max 5; only while the Devout stands; chip counts stacks). Each stack: 3% damage mitigation and +2% damage dealt. At 5 stacks the ally heals 15% max health, Faith resets, and the Devout recovers 3% max Mana.
  • Divine Shield reworked: 15 Mana (was 30), absorbs 50% of the Devout's max health (Perfect: 55%), 2cd (was flat 100/130, 3cd) — the shield is the Faith engine.
  • New — Consecrated Ground: 25 Mana, 3.5, 3cd; the party takes 15% less damage and reflects 10% of damage taken for 2 turns (Perfect: 3).
  • New — Blessing of Zeal: 20 Mana, 2.5, 2cd; an ally gains +15% damage for 3 turns, their cooldowns tick down 1 immediately, and their Faith gain is doubled while it holds (Perfect: 4 turns).
  • Unity renamed Sacred Resolve and talent-gated (same split-damage effect; 25 Mana, 3.0, 5cd). Divine Wrath is VAULTED.
  • New capstone ability — Bulwark of Fortitude: 30 Mana, 3.5, 3cd; for 3 turns the party takes NO Break damage, has its armor increased by 50%, and heals 10% max health per turn (Perfect: instant 5% party heal). Talent-gated.
  • Eighth fixed talent tree — Devout, 12 nodes: Communion (5-stack procs can spread Faith), Unwavering Faith (+0.5%/rank per-stack mitigation and damage), Blessed are the Faithful (5-stack heal 20/25/30%), Devoutness, Afterglow (breaking shields heal 5%/rank of the Devout's health), Sacred Covenant (a shield that prevents lethal damage heals 5%/rank and grants 1 Faith per rank — per the design note, both numbers scale), Radient Aegis (15%/rank shield echo), Blessed Barrier (3%/rank of absorbs become healing), Cleansing Waters (15%/rank per-turn cleanse under Sacred Resolve), Healing Pulse (2%/rank of the Devout's health per turn under Sacred Resolve), plus the two ability nodes.
  • Status renamed: Empowered Divine Plea's debuff ward is now "Hallowed" ("Consecrated" belongs to Consecrated Ground).
  • Verified live, all 12 nodes + kit: shield absorbing 61 (50% of 121), Afterglow 18 on every break, Blessed Barrier conversions, Radient Aegis echoes, a Sacred Covenant LETHAL SAVE (healed 21 and kept the Faith), Communion triple-spread, Healing Pulse + Cleansing Waters under Sacred Resolve, Bulwark's full package, Consecrated Ground reflects, Zeal cooldown ticks, and Conviction overflow (Warden healed 23, Devout +3 Mana); 20-battle sim 20/20, zero errors.

2026-07-22 — Batch 22: Holy talent tree (Resurrection + Divine Plea go live)

  • Seventh fixed talent tree — Holy (from the new tree JSON), 12 nodes: Triage (instant heals can CRIT ×1.5 off the Cleric's crit chance; all healing +3%/rank), Heavenly Aura (each Mercy stack +5%/rank extra healing done), Holy Light (perfect casts restore 1%/rank max Mana), Guardian Angel (the Mercy-earning threshold rises 3%/rank — 53/56/59%), Divine Presence (end of the Cleric's turn: lowest-health ally heals 1%/rank max health), Last Hope (allies under 25% health receive 5%/rank more healing), Inner Faith (+5%/rank max health), Holy Capacitor (33%/rank of overhealing banks on a live chip and is released by the next Heal), On the Mend (Renewal ticks 5%/rank chance to dispel one harmful effect), Sanctified (spending Mercy has a 10%/rank chance to consume no stacks).
  • Resurrection and Divine Plea are now reachable — the tree's two ability nodes grant them (a new "grant_ability" talent payload pulls the defs from Classes, so the tree and the DOD_SIM_ABILITIES hook share one source).
  • Status renamed: Empowered Divine Plea's debuff-immunity ward is now "Consecrated" (was "Sanctified") — the talent owns the Sanctified name.
  • Grid note: the source JSON sat one column right of the standard layout; nodes were normalized onto the usual 3/5/3/1 grid.
  • Verified live, all 12 nodes: Heal "recovers 79 CRIT", Capacitor banking 38→244 and releasing 70 into a Heal, Divine Presence drips, Holy Light +3 Mana on perfects, On the Mend washing a Poison off, Sanctified preserving a spend, Guardian-widened Mercy income, and Divine Plea cast from the TALENT grant; 20-battle talented sim: 18/20, zero errors, 384 healing/battle.

2026-07-22 — Batch 21: Holy Mercy rework, Faith retired, healing scales off caster health

  • Healing rule change: ability healing now scales off the CASTER's max health (shields stay flat). Smite/Shadowrend perfects heal 5% max health (was flat 8).
  • Faith is REMOVED from all Cleric specs, along with the MIRACLE keyword and core Mend Wounds (vaulted). The Cleric core is Smite alone. Resurrection left the core (and Devout/Occultist entirely) — it returns as a Holy talent ability. Unity is now 25 Mana; Umbral Sigil is now 20 Mana (both keep their effects and cooldowns).
  • Holy passive replaced — Grace is gone; enter MERCY (secondary resource, 0–5): +1 stack whenever a party member falls below 50% health; each stack held grants +5% healing done. Stacks are spent on casts (costs are paid up front, so a spender heals with what remains) or on Empowerment — the ✦ Empower toggle (hotkey C) arms the next supporting cast for +1 stack and a bonus effect; Empowered casts forgo their perfect bonus.
  • New Holy kit: Heal — 20 Mana, 3.0, 1cd, heals 40% of the Cleric's max health (Empower: cleanses all harmful effects; Perfect: Cleric recovers 5%). Renewal — now ticks 15% of the CLERIC's max health (snapshotted, Mercy-scaled at cast) for 5 turns (Empower: also blankets the Cleric; Perfect: +5% instantly). Hymn of Hope — now 1 Mercy, 3.5 int, 2cd, heals the party 20% of their own max health (Empower: 35%; Perfect: 25%).
  • Built and waiting on the Holy tree (talent-gated): Resurrection — 3 Mercy, 4.0, 3cd, revive at 20% (Empower: FULL health/resource + 5 turns of Renewal; Perfect: 25%). Divine Plea — 2 Mercy, 3.0, 2cd, fully heals an ally (Empower: cleanse + Sanctified — immune to new debuffs — 3 turns; Perfect: +10 Mana). Until the tree lands they are unreachable; the Revive Potion is the only revive.
  • Arcanist tuning: Resonance's per-stack damage-taken cost drops to +5% (was +10%); Overcharge now affects ONLY the Resonance per-stack values — the Arcane Cannon/Barrage recoil surcharges are gone, and Cannon/Wrath per-stack scalings count raw stacks as before.
  • Fixes: map debug grant is now +200 talent points (was +3); stunned and frozen units now tick their other status timers and cooldowns on the lost turn (timers no longer stall while stun/freeze-locking enemies, e.g. with Enemy attacks OFF); status expiries are logged ("X fades from Y").
  • Tooling: DOD_ENEMIES_OFF=1 arms the enemy-skip debug toggle headlessly; DOD_SIM_ABILITIES="Resurrection,Divine Plea" appends the pending Holy talent abilities to sim bots.
  • Verified live: Mercy income on sub-50% crossings, Heal 51 = 40% × 1.05 with a stack held, Renewal ticking 18 (23 at 5 stacks) with clean 5-tick expiry, Empowered Hymn 37–40%, Empowered Divine Plea full-heal + Sanctified bouncing an incoming Poison, Empowered Resurrection returning a hero at 100% with Renewal; 20-battle sim 20/20, no errors.

2026-07-20 — Batch 20: Arcanist rework + talent tree (Resonance engine), Ice Lance trim

  • Ice Lance damage reduced: 35% of Attack (was 40%).
  • Arcanist core rework — the Resonance engine. Magic Bolt is reforged into Arcane Explosion: free, 10% Atk arcane / 10 BD / 2.0 int, no cd, hits TWO distinct random enemies simultaneously and builds 1 Resonance (2 on a crit) like every damaging cast (no perfect bonus). Death Ray is REMOVED (vaulted — kept for future return).
  • Arcane Cannon retuned: 25 Mana (was 30), 40% Atk (was 30%), 2cd (was 1), BD now scales — 5 × current Resonance stacks (was flat 15); still +7.5% damage per stack and 15% recoil. New perfect: the cast costs 3.0 initiative instead of 3.5 (was +5 BD).
  • Arcane Barrage retuned: 20 Mana (was 25), 6 bolts × 8% Atk (was 5%), 3 BD per bolt (was 4), 2.5 int (was 3.5); bolts now target ONLY the (up to) 3 living enemies with the lowest health, so the volley rakes the 2-3 weakest. Perfect still fires a 7th bolt.
  • New core ability — Stabilize: free, 2.0 int, 3cd; consumes ALL Resonance: +5 Mana and +10% damage reduction (2 turns, "Stabilized" chip) per stack consumed; unusable at 0 stacks (Perfect: also heals 5% max HP). Resonance now persists until Stabilize consumes it.
  • Sixth fixed talent tree — Arcanist (from the new tree JSON), 12 nodes: Mindfulness (every 6/5/4 turns ALL cooldowns tick 1 extra — turns needed drop 1 per point), Arcane Mastery (+1%/rank crit per stack), Mana Attunement (2%/rank max Mana per stack gained), Temporal Rift (crits 3%/rank chance to echo 25% at a random enemy), On the Edge (surviving a hit below 25/30/35% — threshold rises 5 per point — grants 1 Resonance), Conversion (10%/rank of damage taken paid as Mana), Critical Mass (every 3rd crit +20%/rank damage, +10%/rank max Mana), Suppressing Fire (each Barrage bolt +0.25%/rank of Attack over the previous), Unlimited Power (overflow at max stacks → +2%/rank damage and +2%/rank max Mana, battle-long, live chip), Stable Alignment (one attack cannot remove more than 35/30/25% of max health — cap drops 5 per point).
  • New talent-granted abilities: Overcharge — 20 Mana, 2.0 int, 5cd, once per battle: max Resonance becomes 8; stacks 6-8 weigh 1.5× toward the per-stack passive bonuses (damage dealt / crit / damage taken; Perfect: 1.65×); Arcane Cannon +15% recoil and Arcane Barrage gains 15% recoil while active. Magi's Wrath (capstone) — 30 Mana, 15% Atk arcane to ALL enemies, 4.0 int, 4cd; +4% damage per stack, BD = 2.5 × stacks; recoil 15% of damage dealt, −3% per enemy hit (Perfect: costs 3.5 initiative).
  • Supporting changes: the Resonance nameplate readout shows the true (Overcharge-weighted) damage bonus; talent tooltips now render quarter-percent values exactly (Suppressing Fire); the sim bot plays the new rotation (Wrath → Overcharge → Stabilize when hurt at full stacks → Cannon → Barrage); Arcane Explosion's two blasts always pick distinct enemies while two stand.
  • Verified: full-tree autoplay battles — every talent and ability observed proccing in the log (Overcharged Cannon recoil 42 on a 139 hit ✓, Stable Alignment capping a boss slam at 25% max HP ✓, Unlimited Power compounding ✓); 20-battle sim clean.

2026-07-20 — Batch 19: Frostbite status, Blizzard tuning, master doc = current-truth-only

  • New status — Frostbite: the target's healing received is reduced by 50% for 2 turns (a debuff; chip "Fb"). Heal lines and floating numbers show the reduced amount, and enemy heal logs note "halved by Frostbite" when it bites.
  • Rime now also inflicts Frostbite (2 turns) on its target when cast, on top of its chill-spreading mark.
  • Permafrost extended: the Cryomancer's frost attacks have a 25% chance to inflict Frostbite per hit (Frostbolt, Razor Ice, Blizzard, Ice Lance, Shatter), in addition to Frozen enemies taking +15% damage from all sources.
  • Blizzard damage reduced: 15% of Attack per target (was 20%).
  • Talent renamed: the Cryomancer row-1 talent "Frostbite" (Frozen enemies easier to crit) is now "Brittle Ice" — the Frostbite name belongs to the new status. Same node id, so learned ranks carry over.
  • Master Document policy: the master doc now records ONLY what is currently in the game — all change-history language (VAULTED lists, "moved/reworked/no longer" notes, old-vs-new comparisons, decision dates) was swept out; that history lives here in the changelog. Stale facts fixed in the sweep: the zone-multiplier scaling paragraph (node-tier scaling is the real rule), the vaulted Frost Bolt still listed under attacker modifiers, armor-pen examples citing removed abilities (Piercing Arrow, Singularity), and the removed Eidolon Mirror still cited in the Rage rules.
  • Stray text fixes (terminology + stale tooltips): six player-facing strings still said "Pressure" for Break-meter damage — now "Break damage"/"BD" (Ward and Devotion Aura chips, the Devout passive, Mend Wounds and Wildstrikes perfect bonuses, Summon Ursus); the Arcane Barrage tooltip no longer cites the vaulted Echo passive.

2026-07-20 — Batch 18: Cryomancer talent tree (Rime + Shatter), Permafrost, cd toggle

  • Fifth fixed talent tree — Cryomancer (from the new tree JSON), 12 nodes: Hungering Cold (Chilled enemies −1%/rank damage per stack), Frostbite (Frozen +2%/rank easier to crit), Piercing Ice (Ice Lance +10%/rank crit damage), Hypothermia (+1%/rank damage taken per Chilled stack), Frigid Grip (each stack slows 3%/rank harder), Icy Veins (Ice Lance kills empower the next lance +15%/rank), Splintering Shards (Razor Ice 20%/rank chance of an extra target), Whiteout (Blizzard 15%/rank chance to Daze 2 turns), Freezing Advance (Rime spreads sting for 2%/rank of Attack), Empowered Frostbolt (+2%/rank of Attack).
  • New talent-granted abilities: Rime — 25 Mana, 3.0 int, 3cd: for 3 turns, every Chilled stack the target gains also chills one other random enemy (Perfect: 4 turns; spreads never chain). Shatter (capstone) — 30 Mana, 4.0 int, 5cd: every Chilled enemy takes 10% of Attack PER Chilled stack; castable only while someone is Chilled (Perfect: 4cd).
  • Passive changed — Permafrost (replaces Shattering): Frozen enemies take 15% increased damage from all sources.
  • Razor Ice v3: 2 random enemies (was 3), 20% of Attack (was 15%), no more per-stack bonus damage, and every hit now ALWAYS applies a Chilled stack (Perfect: 25% of Attack).
  • Debug: "Reset cooldowns" replaced by a Cooldowns OFF toggle — while checked, hero abilities never go on cooldown (existing cooldowns clear on enabling).
  • Master Document audit: found and fixed — the Rallied status missing from the status table since Batch 12, the Crit keyword still citing the removed "Razor Ice always crits vs Chilled" rule, and the stale Shattering/Razor-v2 Cryomancer block.

2026-07-18 — Batch 17: Cryomancer rework (Control, Chilled stacks → Frozen, Shattering)

  • New archetype — Control (Damage role, 100 Atk): "locks the battlefield down — slows, freezes, and shatters." The Cryomancer is the first Control spec.
  • Chilled reworked into a stacking debuff (Cryomancer-only, max 4): 1 stack −25% speed, 2 stacks −50% speed, 3 stacks −50% speed AND −15% damage dealt; 4 stacks = FROZEN (stacks reset). Lasts 3 turns; every reapplication RESETS the clock; the chip counts stacks. Frozen = loses the next turn (a stun; bosses resist unless Broken).
  • New passive — Shattering (replaces Chill): +2% crit chance for every enemy Frozen this battle, shown on a live chip.
  • Kit rebuilt: core Magic Bolt → Frostbolt (free, 20% Atk frost, 15 BD, 1 Chilled stack; Perfect 25% Atk); Razor Ice v2 (20 Mana, 15% Atk × 3 random, 10 BD, 2.5, 3cd, +3% damage per Chilled stack on the victim; Perfect chills unchilled victims); Blizzard v2 (35 Mana, 20% Atk to ALL, 10 BD, 4.0, 4cd, 1-2 Chilled stacks each; Perfect refunds 5 Mana); NEW Ice Lance (25 Mana, 40% Atk, 15 BD, 3.0, 2cd, always crits vs Frozen; Perfect 20 BD). The old Frost Bolt spear is VAULTED.
  • Verified: 25-battle sim (80% wins, zero errors) with the full kit in rotation — Ice Lance casts confirm freezes trigger; freeze chain observed live in the log.

2026-07-18 — Batch 16: Pyromancer talent tree (Flame Shield + Firestorm)

  • Fourth fixed talent tree — Pyromancer (from the new tree JSON), 12 nodes: Accelerant (Burn ticks +1%/rank of Attack), Pyromaniac (Inferno Master step +1%/rank), Super Nova (Detonation crit +3%/rank), Invigorating Ashes (Burn ticks 5%/rank chance to restore 2% max Mana), Molten Core (2%/rank less damage from burning enemies), Explosive Force (fire crits extend Burn +1 turn/rank), Seeding Embers (enemy dies Burning → +1%/rank damage per remaining Burn turn for the next turn), Melt Armor (Burn ticks melt 1%/rank armor off the victim for the battle — enemy chip with live −%), Ashes of Al'ar (on death: 11%/rank chance to revive at 25% HP, once per battle), Implosion (Detonation 3%/rank chance to strike twice).
  • New talent-granted abilities (only obtainable and visible after unlocking their nodes): Flame Shield — 15 Mana, 2.0 int, 2cd, self-cast: 50% less damage taken for 2 turns and attackers are set Burning 3 turns (Perfect: immediate Burn tick on every burning enemy). Firestorm (capstone) — 30 Mana, 3.5 int, 4cd: 6-8 bolts at random enemies for 12% Atk each, each applying 2 turns of Burn (Perfect: 7-9 bolts).
  • Verified: 25-battle sim with the full tree maxed (84% wins, zero errors, Firestorm/ Flame Shield in rotation); Seeding Embers and Invigorating Ashes procs observed live.

2026-07-16 — Batch 15: Pyromancer rework (Inferno Master + burn engine), strict Adjacent, Burn rule

  • Pyromancer reworked around Burn: passive Ignite replaced by Inferno Master (+5% damage per burning enemy, up to +25%; live chip). Core Magic Bolt reforged into Fireball (free, 20% Atk fire, 15 BD, applies 3 turns of Burn; Perfect hits at 25% Atk). New kit: Detonation (20 Mana, 15% Atk, 20 BD, 2cd — consumes the target's Burn for bonus damage = tick × turns left; Perfect re-applies 1 turn), Wildfire (20 Mana, 20% Atk, 10 BD, 3cd — spreads the target's Burn to Adjacent enemies at half duration), Flamewave (25 Mana, 15% Atk to ALL, 5 BD, 2cd — burning enemies +2 turns, Perfect +3). Removed (vaulted): Pyroblast, Flame Surge, Phoenix Rebirth, and Mana Shield from ALL mage specs.
  • Burn rule finalized: reapplication extends duration — the fresh application's turns ADD to the running timer (was flat +3).
  • Adjacent is strict: a dead neighbor negates the adjacent bonus on that side — splashes (Sundering, Wildfire) never jump past a corpse to the next living enemy.
  • Death Ray has no cooldown — the 5-stack Resonance build-up is the gate. Resurrection is 5cd (was 7).

2026-07-16 — Batch 14: zone-scaled budgets, chiefs elite-only, no enemy cooldowns, Burn stacking

  • Budgets scale across each zone: tiers 1–3 roll 3–6, tiers 4–7 roll 6–9, tiers 8–11 (boss included) roll 10–12. Later zones will restart the ladder with a NEW roster (different names/abilities/higher base stats); the Forest of Old is the current focus. Verified across every tier and node type: all generated warbands spend their band's budget exactly, and Swarm/Poison Volley naturally rule the cheap early tiers.
  • Chiefs appear ONLY in elite fights — removed from the Warband pool and Honor Guard / Rage Company no longer spawn on regular fight nodes (the tier-3 gate is gone as redundant).
  • Enemy cooldowns removed — enemy kits carry no cooldowns; their casts are gated by resources and AI conditions alone. The "bides its time" fallback is removed with them (no enemy can be actionless anymore).
  • Burn stacks duration: every reapplication of Burn extends it by +3 turns ("Burn +3 turns" float).
  • Master Document audit: stale text reconciled — enemy-cd references, the vaulted Bulwark passive in the Constitution list and Break section, "+12 Stability" runes (Poise grants Constitution), the bide-time mechanic, the old flat-6 Burn row, "per rank" phrasing in the status table, and the budget/theme tables brought current.

2026-07-16 — Batch 13: power-budget warbands, scaling retune, mitigation log, Shaman rework

  • Themed encounter generator: every enemy has a power value (Raider 1, Archer 1, Shieldmaster 2, Shaman 2, Chief 4, Boss 7); every battle rolls a budget of 8–12 and spends it EXACTLY. Two-step generation: pick a theme, then fill its roles — 10 themes (Warband, Swarm, Honor Guard, Ritual, Poison Volley, Lightning Storm, Rage Company, Guardian Circle, Elite Patrol, Boss Escort). The field now holds up to 6 enemies; the theme is announced in the combat log. Chiefs stay out of regular fights until global tier 3; elite nodes always roll Chief-led themes, boss nodes always Boss Escort. Verified over 900 generated warbands: every one spends its budget exactly.
  • Scaling retune (linear): heroes +2% of base Attack and +2% of base HP per combat win (was 1/1); enemies +4% of base Attack and +5% of base HP per node tier (was 2/2).
  • Mitigation amounts in the log: hits now report "(nature resist -8)", "(armor -12)", or "(WEAK! +9)" so you can see exactly what defenses ate.
  • All ability cooldowns reduced by 1 turn, game-wide (heroes, talents, enemies) — basics stay 0; Death Ray/Resurrection now 7.
  • Orc Shaman reworked into a true support: new Healing Wave (heals the most wounded ally under 40% HP for 25% of its max HP; friendly tanks and healers first, 2cd); Lightning Bolt VAULTED (removed, kept for future return). Enemies with nothing usable now "bide their time" instead of erroring; enemy configs carry role tags (tank/support/damage) for heal priorities.

2026-07-16 — Batch 12: Attack stat & node scaling, Resistances, Warden tree v2, melee-only parry

  • Attack stat + % scaling: every unit has an Attack stat; every ability's damage is now a PERCENT of the user's current Attack. Base Attack by role: Damage 100 / Tank 75 / Support 50 (per-spec stat blocks coming class by class). Existing damage numbers were converted to preserve current values at base Attack (e.g. Smite 44% of the Cleric's 50). Bruiser moved under the Damage role — the Swordmaster attacks at 100.
  • Node scaling: heroes gain +1% of base Attack and max HP per combat node won (persisted as combat_wins); enemies gain +2% of base per node tier across the whole game (replaces the old ×1.35-per-zone jump). Armor, Resistances, Speed, Constitution, Crit, Block, and Parry never scale. Enemy HP pools are multiples of 10 (rounded up after scaling; bases raised: Raider 140, Archer 110, Chief 250, Tyrant 370).
  • DoTs scale: Burn (6%) and Poison (3%/stack) snapshot the applier's Attack when applied. Companion beasts hit for % of the hunter's Attack (10/20/15%).
  • Resistances (hero stat): six per-element resistances (Fire/Frost/Nature/Holy/ Shadow/Arcane) that work like armor for their type; hover "Resistances" on the stat page. Per-spec values land class by class (0% placeholders today). Orc Shaman: fire 25%, frost 25%, nature 50%.
  • Ability displays: battle tooltips show computed damage from live Attack; the stat page shows damage AND "% of Attack"; the awakening screen shows both; the stat page gains an Attack row (with scaling explanation and win count).
  • Melee-only parry: ranged attackers (Hunter, Mage, Orc Archer, Orc Shaman) can no longer be parried. Parries log their source — reflexes / Sword Mastery / Parry Up — and blocks log theirs — Shieldwall / Heavy Plating / base Block.
  • Warden tree v2 (from the updated tree JSON): Tenacity is live — +5 max HP (battle-long) per Heavy Plating block; Rally is live — party +15% healing received for 2 turns per Heavy Plating block (new "Rallied" chip); Endurance reduced to +1%/rank armor per unhealed turn (was 3%). The Hardiness placeholder is gone.
  • New Warden ability — War Stomp: 20 Rage, 3 shockwaves × 15% Atk / 15 BD to random enemies, 3.0 int, 4cd; allies regain 10% of their resource.
  • Talent tooltips scale with investment: no more "per rank" — the displayed value grows as points go in (3% → 6% → 9%; unlearned nodes preview rank 1).
  • Class passive removed from the draft screen (stays on the awakening screen and party sheet).
  • Saves: loading a run now swaps saved talent-tree snapshots for the live definitions (learned ranks carry over; removed/shrunk nodes refund points), so balance edits reach old saves.

2026-07-16 — Batch 11: talent visibility (chips + logs), Adjacent keyword, debug QoL

  • New keyword — Adjacent: the enemies directly next to a target in formation order (nearest living enemy on each side). Sundering now splashes its Break damage only to enemies Adjacent to the Crushing Blow target, not the whole warband.
  • Buffs are visible: talent effects now show as status chips with live counters — Enraged (+% and stacks), Unrelenting Assault (+Constitution), Endurance (armor streak, clears on external heals), Iron Will (live +% from current debuffs), Crushing Blows (live armor pen from enemy-party bleed), Battle Shout (% gained at cast), Shieldwall (charges count down "SW3"→"SW1"), Elemental Weakness (resist reduction from talent ranks).
  • Unrelenting Assault reworked: the +10 Constitution/rank now lasts 3 turns (was permanent for the battle); still at most once every 5 turns; fades are announced.
  • Buff abilities self-cast: Shieldwall, Hold the Line, and Battle Shout no longer ask for an enemy target.
  • Talent procs are logged: every proc writes a "Talent:" line to the combat log — Enraged, Unrelenting Assault, Endurance, Unkillable, Richocet, Tank and Spank, Dominant Presence, Sundering, Riposte.
  • Latent talent bug fixed: Swordsmanship's bigger perfect-Pommel parry buff was never wired into the parry roll; the parry_up chip now also shows the honed value.
  • Stats screen fixes: Constitution now uses the spec's value and includes Toughness (it previously showed the raw class base); the Seasoned Fighter passive line bakes in Aggressive/Defensive Stance ranks, and Blood Frenzy's line reflects Unstoppable.
  • Class passives displayed: draft cards show the class passive under the name; the awakening screen shows a "Class Passive" line above the three paths.
  • Debug menu: new Reset cooldowns command (wipes everyone's cooldowns, refreshes the action bar). Turn lock reworked: only HERO turns go to the locked hero — enemies keep attacking on their own turns (use Enemy attacks OFF to silence them); displaced heroes' clocks advance as if they had acted.
  • Testing hooks: DOD_SIM_TALENTS="bz_bloodcraze:3,..." force-learns talents on bot heroes; autoplay debug runs echo the combat log to stdout ("[LOG] ..."). Bloodcraze was verified end-to-end with these hooks after a report it wasn't triggering.

2026-07-15 — Batch 10: Block, cooldowns, class passives, three fixed talent trees

  • New keyword — Block: fully negates an attack (damage, BD, effects). Only pure tanks carry a Block stat: the Warden (5% base) and the Orc Shieldmaster (5%). The Warden's new passive Heavy Plating adds +15% chance (replaces Bulwark, vaulted).
  • New keyword — Cooldown (cd): turns before an ability can be reused. Assigned across the entire roster (basics 0, light 2–3, heavy/AoE 3–6, Miracles 5–8, Death Ray/Resurrection 8) — a tuning pass is expected. Buttons show "CD n"; enemies respect cooldowns too. Also defined: Armor Penetration (each % negates a % of armor, not shown on the character screen) and Buff/Debuff (positive/negative statuses).
  • Class passives (all specs of the class): Warrior — Threatening Presence (enemies 20% likelier to attack him); Cleric — Holy Conduit (+15% healing received); Hunter — Tracker (always attacks first); Mage — Evocation (+10 Mana/turn).
  • Warrior reshuffle: Crushing Blow moved Swordmaster → Warden; Pommel Strike has 25% base crit and a crit GUARANTEES its stun; Mocking Blow builds 10 Rage (was 20); Blood Frenzy is stepped (+2% per 5% HP missing); Retaliation and Shieldwall v1 vaulted. New: Sweeping Strikes (Swordmaster core), Battle Shout + reworked Rampage (Berserker talents; Rampage recasts free on kill and chains), Lunge + Execute (Swordmaster talents), Shieldwall v2 (block charges) + Hold the Line (50% less BD + cannot die) (Warden talents).
  • Talent trees are FIXED-only now: designed 12-node grids for Berserker (updated), Swordmaster, and Warden — row gating (5/10/15 points) plus explicit prerequisites with connector lines; ~30 wired effects including on-block heals/stuns, armor-per-debuff, crit-vs-debuffed, counter-on-miss, and bleed-threshold Cripples. All other specs show "Coming soon." (generated trees removed; old saves migrate with refunds). PENDING: Tenacity and Rally reference the undefined "Hardiness" mechanic — learnable but inert until it's designed.

2026-07-12 — Master Document audit

  • Full consistency pass over the Master Document against everything shipped since 07-09: fixed spots where later batches had superseded earlier text — initiative-bar portrait size, the 180px/144px nameplate contradiction, hotkeys described as raw kit slots (they map to menu slots since the summon picker took W), the old "Summon Companion ▸ submenu" wording, "Items ▾" (now the [Alt] Items picker), the Berserker/Pyromancer size-test framing (the test is decided — Pyromancer size is the template), duplicate section number 4.8, and the relic pool count (5). §13 Known Gaps rewritten around the current art pipeline, the unassigned design hooks (Weaknesses, Counter Attack), and the honest sim win rate.

2026-07-12 — Batch 9: parry rework, new Cleric abilities, Alt items, compact UI

  • Sprite template DECIDED: the larger (Pyromancer) size wins — all character art going forward is authored at the 236px/1:1 template. The Berserker's 124px sheets are legacy-sized until re-generated.
  • Parry reworked: a parried hit now lands with 75% less damage and 75% less Break damage instead of being negated — and no longer triggers an automatic counter. Counter Attack is now a defined term: answering a parry with an immediate basic attack, granted only by specific effects (nothing grants it yet).
  • New — Resurrection (all Cleric specs): MIRACLE, 40 Faith, 4.0 int — returns a fallen ally to life with 20% health and resource.
  • New — Divine Wrath (Devout): 25 Mana, 3.0 int — the party deals +15% damage and acts +15% faster for 3 turns (Perfect: 4).
  • New — Umbral Sigil (Occultist): MIRACLE, 20 Faith, 3.0 int — brand one enemy; every other member of its party takes 50% of all attack damage the branded target receives (3 turns, Perfect: 4).
  • Occultist identity: his Smite is corrupted into Shadowrend (same numbers, shadow damage); Mind Flay now costs 30 Mana instead of 25 Faith. Regular Smite stays holy.
  • Tuning & damage types: Overpower costs 30 Rage (was 20); Poisoned Arrow and the Orc Archer's Poison Arrow deal nature damage; Shrapnel Charge deals fire (Explosive Shot already did).
  • Alt — item inventory: Alt toggles an item picker (Tab cycles, Space uses, X closes); the Items button opens the same window. Space continues past victory screens.
  • Compact UI: all battle UI downsized ~20% — nameplates 144px, smaller initiative portraits, log, action bar, popups, pickers, and skill-check bar — the battlefield owns the screen now.
  • Rules clarified in the doc: talent node ranks are additive (every extra point adds the same stated amount).

2026-07-12 — Batch 8: summon picker on W, Pyromancer size-test sprite

  • Summon Companion reworked: it now sits at the TOP of the Beastmaster's ability list on hotkey W. Picking it opens a beast picker window — Tab cycles Ursus / Canis / Aguila (skipping unaffordable ones), Space summons the highlighted beast, X closes, clicks still work. The rest of the kit shifts keys accordingly (Barbed Arrow E, Poisoned Arrow R, Kill Command A). Hotkeys now map to menu slots rather than raw ability indices.
  • Pyromancer test sprite: the east-facing pose from the new imported rotation (file was named "cryomancer test" but the art is the fire-torch Pyromancer) is in-game as a size experiment — native 236px frame at 1:1, untinted, ~55% taller than the Berserker, so the two sprite densities can be compared side by side before locking the character template. Static pose only until animations exist; own-art specs now live in one SPEC_ART table.

2026-07-11 — Batch 7: spec names, portraits, plate polish, X-cancel, walking Berserker

  • Spec names everywhere: once awakened, heroes go by their spec — "Berserker", not "Warrior" — on battle nameplates, the initiative bar, the combat log, and the party screen (list + detail title). The class name only shows pre-awakening. (Internals now key on the class id, so the sim bot and bow sounds are unaffected.)
  • Nameplates polished: ~20% smaller (180px) so the hero stack no longer overlaps, and each plate now carries a portrait — dedicated art when the unit has it (the new Berserker portrait, also used in the initiative bar and party list), else a sprite close-up. The turn indicator is now the plate itself: the acting unit's plate glows with a gold border (the yellow ▼ arrow is gone), and hovering or Tab-targeting a unit lights its plate white.
  • X cancels: whenever a Cancel button is on screen (targeting, cancellable skill check), the X key presses it.
  • Walking Berserker: units with real locomotion art walk to melee range and back using their walk animation (facing the way they move) instead of the abstract lunge — currently the Berserker; new characters get it as their sheets arrive.
  • Summon hotkeys surfaced: the collapsed "Summon Companion ▸" entry shows its beasts' keys ("[W/E/R]") — pressing the key summons directly without opening the submenu.
  • Combat log can hide: a small – button tucks the log away; a "Log" button brings it back.
  • Background at maximum zoom-out: battle art now renders at the smallest scale that still fills the screen. Making the scenery read smaller than this needs background art authored to the Berserker resolution template (next art milestone).

2026-07-11 — Batch 6: nameplates, Tab controls, turn lock, native-res background

  • Nameplates: every unit's name, HP bar, resource bar(s), Break meter, and status chips moved off the sprites onto nameplate stacks behind each party — heroes' plates along the left screen edge, enemies' along the right. Hovering a plate highlights its unit on the field; dead units' plates dim. Both parties are grouped tighter on the field now that the sprites carry no UI. Plate styling is a plain placeholder until the full UI asset set arrives.
  • Tab controls: Tab opens/closes the Abilities list while choosing an action; after an ability is picked, Tab cycles through the available targets and Space/Enter confirms. A full turn is playable from the keyboard: hotkey → Tab → Space → Space on the skill check.
  • Debug turn lock: the battle debug tools moved into a DEBUG ▾ menu (bottom-right) and "Hero Turn Now" was replaced by a proper turn lock — pick a hero and every turn is theirs until you unlock or switch heroes, for testing a kit over many consecutive turns.
  • Background at native size: battle art is no longer overscanned ~30% — it scales to just cover the 1280×720 view (plus a shake margin). The Berserker sheet is the official resolution template going forward (figures ≈60–90px in ~124px frames at ×1.25; backgrounds authored ≈1280×720, shown native).
  • Berserker untinted: heroes with their own art keep original file colors — the draft-slot tint no longer recolors the Berserker.

2026-07-10 — Batch 5: Weaknesses, ability hotkeys, Berserker test sprite, Renewal

  • New mechanic — Weaknesses: a unit can be WEAK to a damage type, taking 25% extra damage from it (stacks with resists; weak-to-nature also amplifies Poison ticks). Weakness hits flash WEAK! over the target and are tagged in the combat log. Who is weak to what will be assigned later — for now the Ash-Wrought Tyrant's frost vulnerability uses the new mechanic.
  • Ability hotkeys: Q / W / E / R / A / S / D / F pick abilities by kit slot during your turn (Q = basic attack), shown on every button ("[W] Bloodlust"). Unaffordable or gated abilities ignore their key; hotkeys work even with the Abilities menu open, but never during targeting or skill checks.
  • Berserker test sprite: the three GIFs from imported files (idle / move / attack) are converted into game sheets — a Warrior specced Berserker now fights with the fur-cloaked axeman in battle. Native 124px frames at 1.25× scale, feet aligned with the party line. Missing clips fall back gracefully (Attack02/03 replay Attack01; Hurt/Death hold the idle frame) until those animations arrive.
  • Renewal is Holy-only (leaves the Cleric core kit) and heals 15 HP per turn (was 8); a Perfect cast now also heals 15 instantly (was 8). Still 20 Mana, 5 turns, 3.0 initiative.

2026-07-09 — Batch 4: new orcs, Warden rework, Dazed, encounter + combat rules

  • New enemy — Orc Shieldmaster (orange): 150 HP / 25% armor / 85 speed / 120 Constitution. Shielding wards one ally at a time (lowest HP first, itself included) with 25% damage reduction for 3 turns — it ALWAYS re-casts when nobody holds the ward; otherwise Strike (28 physical / 20 BD). New "Shielded" status chip.
  • New enemy — Orc Shaman (blue): 110 HP / 1% armor / 100 speed / 80 Constitution, ranged. Lightning Bolt (35 nature / 20 BD) or Chain Lightning (15 nature + 15 BD to the whole party).
  • Withered Warden reworked (zone 1 boss, no longer a Chief stand-in): 500 HP / 35% armor / 75% nature resist / 80 speed / 150 Constitution. Timber Slam (60 nature / 30 BD + Dazed), Roots of Wrath (25 nature / 20 BD to the whole party + Poison), Wild Growth (heals the most wounded of its warband for 20% max HP; 60% chance when someone is under 70%).
  • New status — Dazed: attacks are 20% more likely to miss, 3 turns.
  • Encounters: every fight now fields 3–5 enemies with types rolled at random from the basic pool (Raider / Archer / Shieldmaster / Shaman). Elites (Chief) appear only on elite nodes, bosses only on the boss node (each brings 2–4 random basics).
  • Combat rules: enemy parry baseline is now 2.5% (heroes stay at 5%); bleedout damage explicitly ignores armor; Poison now counts as nature damage, so nature resists shrink the ticks (the Warden barely feels it).
  • Hero info screen: each stat now explains itself when hovered (instead of one tooltip dumping every stat); the talent-point line left the stats block (the count already sits above the talent tree); the requirements text below the talent tree is gone (lock reasons still show on node tooltips).
  • Battle debug panel: per-hero "Turn →" buttons replaced by one Hero Turn Now button (the next turn goes to a hero regardless of order) plus an Enemy attacks OFF toggle (enemies skip their turns while checked). New DOD_SIM_ENEMIES env var forces the enemy lineup in test battles.
  • Docs: the Word exports (Master Document + Changelog) now build in Arial, size 14.

2026-07-09 — Batch 3: spec-save fix, always-on dev debug, Death Ray gate

  • Fix — reroll reverting talents: the run was never saved after spec confirmation, so quitting before completing another node made Continue resurrect the old specs and trees (this is what made the Berserker tree "revert"). Specs now save immediately on confirmation and on debug reroll, and loading migrates old saves to current fixed trees (points refunded).
  • Fix — missing battle debug menu: debug tools were gated behind the DOD_DEBUG env var, which was only set when launched from the assistant. They are now always available in dev (unexported) builds — exported releases still hide them.
  • Death Ray: castable only at 5 Arcane Resonance stacks (greyed out otherwise); its 150 damage is a fixed payoff — the per-stack damage bonus no longer double-dips on it.

2026-07-09 — Batch 2: talent tree styling, battle debug panel, Arcanist identity

  • Talent tree restyled to match the "Appearance example" reference: dark backdrop with faint grid lines, 72px square emblem nodes (gold border on hover, green when maxed), point counters beneath each node, black hover tooltip with white bold name + yellow description, click-to-learn. Backdrop and node icons are placeholders for future art.
  • Battle debug panel (DOD_DEBUG=1, under the combat log): Full Restore (HP/resources/secondaries) and per-hero "Turn →" buttons to hand any hero the next turn.
  • Fix — Poison instant procs: the "no tick on the turn applied" rule was never wired into the damage loop, so Poisoned Arrow's stacks all ticked immediately on the victim's next turn start. Poison now genuinely skips its first tick.
  • Fix — Explosive Shot BD readout: the log printed the ability's base BD while the meter moved by the post-Constitution amount (85-Constitution archers take ~18% more, 130+ chiefs less — that's the per-enemy variation, by design; crits also deal ×1.5 BD). Logs now report the applied BD so the numbers always match the meter.
  • Arcane Resonance is now the Arcanist's passive. The Mage class no longer has the Resonance bar — only the Arcanist spec gains it (Pyromancer/Cryomancer never see it). The Echo passive is vaulted (removed, kept for later).
  • New — Death Ray (Arcanist): free cast, 150 arcane damage / 100 BD / 8.0 int; benefits from Resonance, then CONSUMES every stack. Perfect restores 15 Mana.

2026-07-09 — Batch 1: fixes, tuning pass, Mana Shield, Berserker tree

  • Fixes: every skill check can now be cancelled, including after picking a target (the "healing wave" case); enemy targeting spreads out — 40% chance to hunt the weakest (was 65%), so the Mage stops eating every hit; rune equip logic was verified working across multiple heroes simultaneously (data + save round-trip tested) — equipped runes now show a green ✦ marker for clarity; Poison no longer ticks on the turn it lands; the Cryomancer's Chilled is now its own status separate from generic Slowed (same −25% speed); Eidolon Mirror removed from the relic pool (existing saves drop it safely).
  • Balance: all enemies +15% HP (Raider 132, Archer 104, Chief 242, bosses 368); Cripple now −25% damage (was −15%).
  • Terminology: damage against the Break meter is now Break damage (BD) in all tooltips and documentation.
  • Ability tuning: Overpower 15 base damage (still +0.5 per point of target Break), Perfect gives +5 Rage; Crushing Blow 20 BD, Perfect +5 BD; Flame Surge 15 damage / 10 BD / 3.0 int, no longer applies Burn, Perfect +15 damage to already-Burning enemies; Echo now strikes at 25% power (was 50%); Arcane Cannon 15 BD, recoil is a flat 15% of damage dealt (no per-stack scaling), Perfect +5 BD; Quick Shot and Powershot lose their +10 Focus perfects; Barbed Arrow and Poisoned Arrow 15 BD / 2.5 int; Aimed Shot 45 damage / 15 BD / 3.0 int, Perfect +25% crit; Powershot 3.0 int with plainer wording; Mocking Blow 20 damage / 15 BD / 2.0 int, builds 20 Rage, taunts the target AND one other enemy for 4 turns (Perfect 5); Shieldwall 3.0 int, party takes 25% less damage for 2 turns (Perfect 3); Summon Canis 20 damage / 20 Bleed; Tripwire tooltip simplified; Shrapnel renamed Shrapnel Charge — 20 damage / 25 BD, applies Slowed (not Chilled).
  • New — Mana Shield (Mage core, replacing Arcane Surge): 15 Mana, 2.0 int, for 3 turns 50% of damage the Mage takes converts into Mana; Perfect casts at 1.5 int. Arcane Surge and Reality Fracture are vaulted (removed from kits, definitions kept for later).
  • Berserker talent tree v2 — built from the RPG Skill Tree Generator export (data/berserker_tree.json): a fixed 3×3 tree. Tier 1: Savagery (+2 Bleed on bleed abilities /rank ×3), Bloodthirsty (Blood Frenzy +3%/rank ×3), Vitality (+5% max HP/rank ×3). Tier 2: Crushing Force (ignore 5% armor/rank ×3), Rampage (new ability: +1% damage per 10 Bleed buildup on the enemy party for 1 turn, 20 Rage), Reckless Fury (+5% damage dealt AND taken /rank ×3). Tier 3: Bloodlust (Hack and Slash strikes an extra time), Bloodcraze (heal 30 when an enemy bleeds out), Enraged (+1%/rank damage per hit taken, fades after 2 unhurt turns ×3). Gating: Tier 2 unlocks at 5 points in Tier 1; Tier 3 at 10 points across Tiers 1–2. The party screen renders fixed trees three columns wide.
  • Docs: Master Document converted through a new build script that unwraps paragraphs (fixes the strange spacing in Word); this changelog created alongside it.
